--- entity_google_reviews [row 2 of 5] ---
review_id: 8616
entity_slug: alice-mama-thai-cafe
google_review_name: places/ChIJXWmxznXbkIgREWwWZ75-_JI/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT2s1WFkxQktWVmRKVDNjd2IzWkpWR3B1UzB0eU1tYxAB
rating: 4
review_text: While staying in Gulf Breeze for the week, my wife and I decided to try out Alice Mama Thai Cafe. We ordered our food for take out, which was ready 15 minutes earlier than expected. No real interaction with the server except for telling her which order I was picking up. The restaurant itself was small, but had ample seating if one wanted to dine in.

Now to the food. We ordered the following:
- Crab Rangoon
- Panang Curry with Tofu, Spice Level = Spicy, which also comes with white rice.
- Pad Noodle Prik Gaeng with Shrimp, Spice Level = Spicy

First thing I want to state is that this place appears to give true spice levels. There are plenty of places that say they give spicy, but the actual spice is little to none. That’s not the case here. Both the noodle and curry dishes had great heat.

Next thing is the flavor: Every dish had ample flavor and tasted how I would expect. I’ve never had Pad Noodle Prik Gaeng before, but I will say it tasted phenomenal. The kaffir lime leaves and basil really came out in the red curry. With the Panang Curry, it tasted rich and creamy. The crab rangoon was also made extremely well. Perfect mixture of cream cheese and crab flavor. The little char was also nice.

Overall, I recommend anyone to give this place a try. I would definitely eat here again.

id: 7d70b248-e415-48bf-9e47-890ee5fc18c4
entity_slug: alice-mama-thai-cafe
reviewer_name: Patricia Finney
rating: 5
body: Fantastic! So flavorful! We were the first customers at lunch and it was fun watching the serve expertly handle each customer as all tables filled within 30 minutes. And she serviced everyone, including call in orders. I  thought the server’s name was Pang so I edited this post as we ate there again today and a different, wonderful server took care of us. When I looked at the ticket, it said Pang. I asked and she said that is owners name . We got a good laugh! My wife had the pad thai and we both loved it. I was happy to see my favorite on the menu: Nam Tok. We will be back!!!Update: added photos of today. Shrimp tempura was best we’ve EVER had, pot stickers were wonderful and the regular Thai Beef salad and Pad Pak very delicious as well. Love this place. And I’ve beeb to many Thai restaurants.

--- entity_reviews [row 2 of 5] ---
id: 13732189-0caa-466a-9a98-987f1f732d77
entity_slug: alice-mama-thai-cafe
reviewer_name: Matthew Reyes
rating: 4
body: While staying in Gulf Breeze for the week, my wife and I decided to try out Alice Mama Thai Cafe. We ordered our food for take out, which was ready 15 minutes earlier than expected. No real interaction with the server except for telling her which order I was picking up. The restaurant itself was small, but had ample seating if one wanted to dine in.

Now to the food. We ordered the following:
- Crab Rangoon
- Panang Curry with Tofu, Spice Level = Spicy, which also comes with white rice.
- Pad Noodle Prik Gaeng with Shrimp, Spice Level = Spicy

First thing I want to state is that this place appears to give true spice levels. There are plenty of places that say they give spicy, but the actual spice is little to none. That’s not the case here. Both the noodle and curry dishes had great heat.

Next thing is the flavor: Every dish had ample flavor and tasted how I would expect. I’ve never had Pad Noodle Prik Gaeng before, but I will say it tasted phenomenal. The kaffir lime leaves and basil really came out in the red curry. With the Panang Curry, it tasted rich and creamy. The crab rangoon was also made extremely well. Perfect mixture of cream cheese and crab flavor. The little char was also nice.

Overall, I recommend anyone to give this place a try. I would definitely eat here again.
